Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..20)
find y: int(0..5)
find z: int(0..6)

such that

!((x / (y / z)) = 10)

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x

  find x#sign_magnitude_1: bool

  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_5,x#sign_magnitude_6;int(1..)] [5, 20]) >= 5,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [x#sign_magnitude_1,x#sign_magnitude_2,x#sign_magnitude_3,x#sign_magnitude_4,x#sign_magnitude_5,x#sign_magnitude_6;int(1..)] [5, 20]) <= 20;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
